# Library map

Generated by `tools/progress/gen.py` from `tools/progress/data/` (refresh with `tools/progress/sync.py`). Upstream: TomBox `7e8064f`; emulator source paths link to upstream. Titles analysed: Fortnite (console OS 10.0.19041.3948), XDefiant (console OS 10.0.22000.4429), Terraria (console OS 10.0.22000.4429), Trackmania (console OS 10.0.22621.1852), Minecraft (no console OS dump; build 22000 assumed from 10.0.22000.3160).

## Scope: Xbox console libraries only

The map counts only what the console provides and a PC does not. A console export is console-related when this PC's `C:\Windows\System32` has no DLL of the same name, or has one that does not export that name (for an export by ordinal only: that ordinal). A console DLL that is a modified PC DLL (kernel32, advapi32, mfplat, wldp, ...) therefore counts only for the exports the PC DLL lacks; console DLLs whose every export exists in the PC DLL of the same name (ntdll, msvcrt, ucrtbase, combase, xgameruntime, GameInput, ...) are left out. Title imports are counted the same way: imports that a PC System32 DLL or PC API set provides, and imports from DLLs shipped with the title, are excluded from both done and total. Absence from System32 is the only test, so a few non-Xbox components that happen not to ship with desktop Windows (symsrv, ConIoSrv, KernelTraceControl, the Media Foundation H.264/HEVC decoder DLLs) are counted. Four console host DLLs have no same-named PC DLL but a differently named PC twin (minuserx -> user32, kernel32legacy -> kernel32 / kernelbase, advapi32legacy -> advapi32, minappmodelx -> twinapi.appcore / kernel.appcore); they stay counted in full, and while one is not emulated only its title-imported exports that the twin exports by name are marked `pc` (or `hooked`), so the denominator does not change. kernel32legacy is not treated as a twin: it is emulated by its own compat DLL (crates/compat/kernel32legacy, console ordinals, kernel32-legacy API-set imports routed to it), and every row takes its status from that crate's exports.txt (exports_19041.txt for a title on console OS 19041). Title imports whose console API-set host is one of these four DLLs are counted against that host, not as PC imports.

## Statuses

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| implemented | the emulator provides its own implementation |
| forwarded | an emulator DLL exports it and forwards to a PC system component (D3D12, Media Foundation, DirectStorage) |
| hooked | PC-twin row (below) that the emulator's OS host also hooks |
| pc | console host DLL with no same-named PC DLL and no emulator crate (minuserx, advapi32legacy, minappmodelx when not emulated): a title imports the export and its PC twin exports the same name; not implemented by the emulator |
| stub | the emulator exports it but returns a fixed result (E_NOTIMPL / S_OK / inert object) |
| unverified | imported by a binary or module that was never observed loading |
| missing | no emulator export, and no PC System32 export of that name |

Shader compile: all 139 shaders of the lobby frame compile with DXC (TomBox `docs/DEVELOPMENT_LOG.md:894`); 25/25 fixtures compile (`:672`). The full-library compile sweep (`:706`) has no published final count, so no compile badge is shown.

Direct3D 12.x: 160 / 128 are the table sizes the wrappers allocate (`d3d12_xs/src/device_wrap.rs`, `cmdlist_wrap.rs`), not documented interface sizes. Device: done = forwarded PC slots 0-43 plus intercepted Xbox slots 44, 48, 52 (SetDebugFlags_X, documented no-op), 55, 56/57 and 66/67 (graphics/compute PSO serialize/deserialize, emulator TBPS format only), 60-62, 73, 78-81, 87 (OpenExistingHeapFromAddress via ID3D12Device3); partial = logging S_OK stubs in 45-90, including 74 (RegisterPagePoolX, bookkeeping only). CommandList: done = 0-26, 32-52 (PC methods shifted by 4), 59, 67, 68, 83; 27-31 are Xbox-inserted stubs and 53-127 log-and-ignore probes, counted as not done.

Direct3D 12.x on Xbox One (Terraria, Trackmania; `crates/compat/d3d12_x`): the console `d3d12_x.dll` is a 48 KB loader (export table identical in both dumps, 54 exports, ordinals 1-54) that loads a user-mode driver flavour (`xgs12.dll`, `xgs12_d.dll`, `xgs12_i.dll`) and calls its `UMDCreateDevice12`; Scarlett's `d3d12_xs.dll` loads `xgs12_10*.dll` instead. The COM vtables live in those drivers, not in `d3d12_x.dll`. Neither driver carries RTTI type names for the D3D12 classes. Tables found statically (a constructor `lea` of an `.rdata` table of code pointers, inside a `.pdata` function) number 109 in `xgs12.dll` and 67 in `xgs12_10.dll`; the only one tied to an interface IID through its QueryInterface is ID3D12CommandQueue: 34 slots on Xbox One, 35 on Scarlett. The ID3D12Device and ID3D12GraphicsCommandList tables were not identified by static analysis: both drivers hold long unattributed runs of static code pointers (about 480 entries in `xgs12.dll`, 520 in `xgs12_10.dll`) that may contain them, or the tables may be assembled at run time. So the Xbox One layout of the Xbox-specific slots (Device 44+, CommandList 27-31 and 53+) is unverified. From the titles' own call sites (device pointer traced from the `D3D12XboxCreateDevice` output: Trackmania `[renderer+0x3e80]`, Terraria a global; then `mov rax,[dev]; call [rax+8*n]`), the device slots the titles call are: Trackmania 2, 13, 15, 18-20, 52, 53, 59, 80; Terraria 0, 2, 9, 10, 13-16, 18, 19, 22, 25, 27-30, 36-38, 41, 48, 62, 64, 65, 68, 69, 73, 79-81. Xbox-range slots used by an Xbox One title are therefore 48, 52, 53, 59, 62, 64, 65, 68, 69, 73, 79, 80, 81; their meaning on Xbox One is not yet matched to the Scarlett slots of the same index (Trackmania calls 53 with no arguments and feeds its result to 52 right after creation). Both titles fill the first dword of the 0x28-byte create-parameter block with 0x1000a. The interface IIDs are unchanged (`IID_ID3D12Device` 189819f1-..., the PC GUID), the `D3D12XboxCreateDevice` parameter block is 0x28 bytes and NULL is rejected with E_INVALIDARG. The emulator therefore forwards Xbox One device creation to `d3d12_xs.dll` (Scarlett wrapper): PC slots 0-43 behave as on PC; any Xbox-only slot a title calls goes to the Scarlett slot of the same index, which may be a different method. `D3DMapEsramMemory(Flags, VA, NumPages, PageList)` (signature from the console stub, 0x18-byte driver packet) accepts flags 1 and 3 only and is mapped onto ordinary committed memory of NumPages x 64 KiB at the caller's VA. Xbox One command-list slots seen in Trackmania's recorded lists (`TOMBOX_CMDLIST_SPY`, 11-21) take arguments consistent with the Scarlett mapping: 9 Close, 10 Reset (allocator, null), 21 RSSetViewports (1, ptr), 22 RSSetScissorRects (1, ptr), 26 ResourceBarrier (n, ptr; Xbox state bit 0x400000 OR'd into SR states, stripped for PC), 29 EndQuery (heap, TIMESTAMP, index), 30 ResolveQueryData (heap, TIMESTAMP, start, ...), 32 SetDescriptorHeaps (2, ptr), 50 OMSetRenderTargets (1, ptr, ...), 52 ClearRenderTargetView (handle, color, 0 rects), 83 texture copy (ptr). Xbox One CreateCommittedResource: upload-heap states (0x8c3) become GENERIC_READ, readback states COPY_DEST, and DENY_SHADER_RESOURCE without ALLOW_DEPTH_STENCIL is dropped; ID3D12Resource::GetGPUVirtualAddress on a committed texture returns its CPU-visible backing (unified memory, Unity uses it as the texel pointer), mirrored into the PC texture through the `xg_x` layout.

## Fortnite imports (2.0.1461.7919)

Every static import of the EXE and its statically loaded DLLs resolved at load: the process booted and presented frames in the 2026-11-21 runs. Delay imports resolve only when first called.

Listed: imports from Xbox console libraries only. Not listed: 1168 imports a PC System32 DLL or API set provides, 356 imports from DLLs shipped with the title.

Here: os_host::plm::register(AppState) in the owner; plm::suspend/resume (and the suspend timeout watch in os_host::quiesce) deliver on a separate notification thread, one registration inline and several in parallel threads joined before the signal returns. VOID (PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION Registration) on the console; the exported function returns a ULONG here (0, or 0x490 ERROR_NOT_FOUND) which a VOID caller ignores. Core: if never initialised returns at once; else under the lock finds the entry whose id (+0x30) matches in this kind's list (an id of the other kind is not found: wil log, 0x490), unlinks it, leaves the lock, then WaitOnAddress until the entry's active-thread field (+0x3c) is 0 or the calling thread (so an in-flight callback finishes first, and unregistering from inside the callback does not deadlock), drops the reference, and when both lists are empty unsubscribes the WNF names (RtlUnsubscribeWnfNotificationWaitForCompletion) and clears the init flag. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(Constrained) in the owner, same find/unlink/wait-for-in-flight behaviour; after it returns no further callback is made for that registration) |
| `RegisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f80 in the Fortnite image (file version 10.0.19041.3948). XDefiant image (10.0.22000.4429): thunk 0x5ad0 to register core 0x56ac, spot-checked to the same shape (*Registration = 0, 0x48-byte entry, shared counter increment, *Registration = id, return 0) and the same four WNF state names; its unregister and dispatch cores were not re-walked, and it imports RtlWaitOnAddress/RtlWakeAddressAll/NtQueryWnfStateData where Fortnite imports WaitOnAddress/WakeByAddressAll/RtlQueryWnfStateData. Fortnite thunk 0x4f80 -> register core 0x4b80 with the constrained list (.data 0xa930). Signature ULONG (PAPPCONSTRAIN_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION* Registration). Core: *Registration = 0 first, then under the module critical section (0xa900) a first-use init that subscribes the four PLM WNF state names (0x4a1c: RtlQueryWnfStateData then RtlSubscribeWnfStateChangeNotification, error -> RtlNtStatusToDosError returned); allocates a 0x48-byte entry (refcount 1 at +0x38, routine +0x20, context +0x28, id +0x30) or returns 8 (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); the id is one process-wide counter (0xa8a0) shared by both registration kinds, starting at 1; entry appended to the list tail; *Registration = id; returns 0. Here: compat-common os_host::plm::register(Constrained) in the os_host owner module (all compat DLLs must be built from the same compat-common so the owner publishes the register entry) (cross-module through TOMBOX_OSHOST_PLM_REGISTER), the same id counter, list and refcount/active-thread scheme. The emulator PLM signals (window minimise/restore watcher, TOMBOX_PLM_DIR command file, plm::set_constrained) stand in for the WNF names 0x41c60d2aa3bc2875 (constrained TRUE) and 0x41c60d2aa3bc3075 (constrained FALSE). Known deviation: the console writes *Registration without a NULL check (a NULL pointer faults); here NULL returns 87 (ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) and registers nothing) |

| `RegisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f40 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5a90) -> the same register core 0x4b80 with the app-state list (.data 0xa988). Signature ULONG (PAPPSTATE_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPSTATE_REGISTRATION* Registration); routine is VOID (BOOLEAN Quiesced, PVOID Context). Same core as ordinal 1: shared id counter, entry appended, *Registration = id, returns 0. Delivery (WNF callback 0x48f0 -> dispatch 0x4790): WNF 0x41c60d2aa3bc1875 -> Quiesced = TRUE (suspend), 0x41c60d2aa3bc2075 -> Quiesced = FALSE (resume); every registration of that kind is snapshotted under the lock, addref'd and marked with the delivering thread id (+0x3c); one registration is called directly on the notification thread (0x45cc), several are fanned out to threadpool work items in a cleanup group and waited for (0x4650), falling back to sequential calls if the threadpool fails. Here: os_host::plm::register(AppState) in the owner; plm::suspend/resume (and the suspend timeout watch in os_host::quiesce) deliver on a separate notification thread, one registration inline and several in parallel threads joined before the signal returns. Same NULL-Registration deviation as ordinal 1) |
| `UnregisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f60 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5ab0) -> the same unregister core 0x4dd8 with the app-state list; same behaviour and the same ULONG-for-VOID note as ordinal 3. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(AppState) in the owner) |

</details>

| `RegisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f80 in the Fortnite image (file version 10.0.19041.3948). XDefiant image (10.0.22000.4429): thunk 0x5ad0 to register core 0x56ac, spot-checked to the same shape (*Registration = 0, 0x48-byte entry, shared counter increment, *Registration = id, return 0) and the same four WNF state names; its unregister and dispatch cores were not re-walked, and it imports RtlWaitOnAddress/RtlWakeAddressAll/NtQueryWnfStateData where Fortnite imports WaitOnAddress/WakeByAddressAll/RtlQueryWnfStateData. Fortnite thunk 0x4f80 -> register core 0x4b80 with the constrained list (.data 0xa930). Signature ULONG (PAPPCONSTRAIN_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION* Registration). Core: *Registration = 0 first, then under the module critical section (0xa900) a first-use init that subscribes the four PLM WNF state names (0x4a1c: RtlQueryWnfStateData then RtlSubscribeWnfStateChangeNotification, error -> RtlNtStatusToDosError returned); allocates a 0x48-byte entry (refcount 1 at +0x38, routine +0x20, context +0x28, id +0x30) or returns 8 (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); the id is one process-wide counter (0xa8a0) shared by both registration kinds, starting at 1; entry appended to the list tail; *Registration = id; returns 0. Here: compat-common os_host::plm::register(Constrained) in the os_host owner module (all compat DLLs must be built from the same compat-common so the owner publishes the register entry) (cross-module through TOMBOX_OSHOST_PLM_REGISTER), the same id counter, list and refcount/active-thread scheme. The emulator PLM signals (window minimise/restore watcher, TOMBOX_PLM_DIR command file, plm::set_constrained) stand in for the WNF names 0x41c60d2aa3bc2875 (constrained TRUE) and 0x41c60d2aa3bc3075 (constrained FALSE). Known deviation: the console writes *Registration without a NULL check (a NULL pointer faults); here NULL returns 87 (ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) and registers nothing) |
| `UnregisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4fa0 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5af0) -> unregister core 0x4dd8 with the constrained list. VOID (PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION Registration) on the console; the exported function returns a ULONG here (0, or 0x490 ERROR_NOT_FOUND) which a VOID caller ignores. Core: if never initialised returns at once; else under the lock finds the entry whose id (+0x30) matches in this kind's list (an id of the other kind is not found: wil log, 0x490), unlinks it, leaves the lock, then WaitOnAddress until the entry's active-thread field (+0x3c) is 0 or the calling thread (so an in-flight callback finishes first, and unregistering from inside the callback does not deadlock), drops the reference, and when both lists are empty unsubscribes the WNF names (RtlUnsubscribeWnfNotificationWaitForCompletion) and clears the init flag. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(Constrained) in the owner, same find/unlink/wait-for-in-flight behaviour; after it returns no further callback is made for that registration) |

</details>

Here: os_host::plm::unregister(AppState) in the owner) |

Signature ULONG (PAPPSTATE_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPSTATE_REGISTRATION* Registration); routine is VOID (BOOLEAN Quiesced, PVOID Context). Same core as ordinal 1: shared id counter, entry appended, *Registration = id, returns 0. Delivery (WNF callback 0x48f0 -> dispatch 0x4790): WNF 0x41c60d2aa3bc1875 -> Quiesced = TRUE (suspend), 0x41c60d2aa3bc2075 -> Quiesced = FALSE (resume); every registration of that kind is snapshotted under the lock, addref'd and marked with the delivering thread id (+0x3c); one registration is called directly on the notification thread (0x45cc), several are fanned out to threadpool work items in a cleanup group and waited for (0x4650), falling back to sequential calls if the threadpool fails. Here: os_host::plm::register(AppState) in the owner; plm::suspend/resume (and the suspend timeout watch in os_host::quiesce) deliver on a separate notification thread, one registration inline and several in parallel threads joined before the signal returns. Same NULL-Registration deviation as ordinal 1) |

</details>

## Terraria imports (4.4.14.1081)

Unity 2021.3.16f1 GameCore **Xbox One** player (`TargetDeviceFamily="XboxOne"`, PDB `GameCoreXboxOnePlayerNoDevelopment_Master`), IL2CPP scripting backend (no CLR header; C# compiled to `Data/Native/GameAssembly.dll` + `global-metadata.dat`), FMOD audio over XAudio2, Unity `baselib.dll`. Scanned: `Mount` (Terraria.exe, XCurl, baselib, MSVC 14.28 CRT), `Mount/Data/Native` (GameAssembly + MSVC 14.29 CRT), `Mount/Data/Plugins` (PartyWin, PartyXboxLive, PlayFabMultiplayerGDK, TerrariaNative, XCurl). Imports `d3d12_x.dll` / `xg_x.dll` / `XFrontPanelDisplay.dll` (the Xbox One variants, not the Scarlett `_xs` ones); the emulator provides them as separate crates with the console's export ordinals, and the remaining gaps are stubs. XGameRuntime is loaded by name (`xgameruntime.dll` + `QueryApiImpl`), so its services are not in the import table.

Static analysis only: this title has not been run.

Listed: imports from Xbox console libraries only. Not listed: 2558 imports a PC System32 DLL or API set provides, 877 imports from DLLs shipped with the title.

| Function | Status | Implemented in / evidence |

| `UnregisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f60 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5ab0) -> the same unregister core 0x4dd8 with the app-state list; same behaviour and the same ULONG-for-VOID note as ordinal 3. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(AppState) in the owner) |
| `RegisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f40 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5a90) -> the same register core 0x4b80 with the app-state list (.data 0xa988). Signature ULONG (PAPPSTATE_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPSTATE_REGISTRATION* Registration); routine is VOID (BOOLEAN Quiesced, PVOID Context). Same core as ordinal 1: shared id counter, entry appended, *Registration = id, returns 0. Delivery (WNF callback 0x48f0 -> dispatch 0x4790): WNF 0x41c60d2aa3bc1875 -> Quiesced = TRUE (suspend), 0x41c60d2aa3bc2075 -> Quiesced = FALSE (resume); every registration of that kind is snapshotted under the lock, addref'd and marked with the delivering thread id (+0x3c); one registration is called directly on the notification thread (0x45cc), several are fanned out to threadpool work items in a cleanup group and waited for (0x4650), falling back to sequential calls if the threadpool fails. Here: os_host::plm::register(AppState) in the owner; plm::suspend/resume (and the suspend timeout watch in os_host::quiesce) deliver on a separate notification thread, one registration inline and several in parallel threads joined before the signal returns. Same NULL-Registration deviation as ordinal 1) |

</details>

<details><summary><b>api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll</b>: 2 functions; implemented 2</summary>

| Function | Status | Implemented in / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `RegisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f80 in the Fortnite image (file version 10.0.19041.3948). XDefiant image (10.0.22000.4429): thunk 0x5ad0 to register core 0x56ac, spot-checked to the same shape (*Registration = 0, 0x48-byte entry, shared counter increment, *Registration = id, return 0) and the same four WNF state names; its unregister and dispatch cores were not re-walked, and it imports RtlWaitOnAddress/RtlWakeAddressAll/NtQueryWnfStateData where Fortnite imports WaitOnAddress/WakeByAddressAll/RtlQueryWnfStateData. Fortnite thunk 0x4f80 -> register core 0x4b80 with the constrained list (.data 0xa930). Signature ULONG (PAPPCONSTRAIN_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION* Registration). Core: *Registration = 0 first, then under the module critical section (0xa900) a first-use init that subscribes the four PLM WNF state names (0x4a1c: RtlQueryWnfStateData then RtlSubscribeWnfStateChangeNotification, error -> RtlNtStatusToDosError returned); allocates a 0x48-byte entry (refcount 1 at +0x38, routine +0x20, context +0x28, id +0x30) or returns 8 (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); the id is one process-wide counter (0xa8a0) shared by both registration kinds, starting at 1; entry appended to the list tail; *Registration = id; returns 0. Here: compat-common os_host::plm::register(Constrained) in the os_host owner module (all compat DLLs must be built from the same compat-common so the owner publishes the register entry) (cross-module through TOMBOX_OSHOST_PLM_REGISTER), the same id counter, list and refcount/active-thread scheme. The emulator PLM signals (window minimise/restore watcher, TOMBOX_PLM_DIR command file, plm::set_constrained) stand in for the WNF names 0x41c60d2aa3bc2875 (constrained TRUE) and 0x41c60d2aa3bc3075 (constrained FALSE). Known deviation: the console writes *Registration without a NULL check (a NULL pointer faults); here NULL returns 87 (ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) and registers nothing) |
| `UnregisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4fa0 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5af0) -> unregister core 0x4dd8 with the constrained list. VOID (PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION Registration) on the console; the exported function returns a ULONG here (0, or 0x490 ERROR_NOT_FOUND) which a VOID caller ignores. Core: if never initialised returns at once; else under the lock finds the entry whose id (+0x30) matches in this kind's list (an id of the other kind is not found: wil log, 0x490), unlinks it, leaves the lock, then WaitOnAddress until the entry's active-thread field (+0x3c) is 0 or the calling thread (so an in-flight callback finishes first, and unregistering from inside the callback does not deadlock), drops the reference, and when both lists are empty unsubscribes the WNF names (RtlUnsubscribeWnfNotificationWaitForCompletion) and clears the init flag. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(Constrained) in the owner, same find/unlink/wait-for-in-flight behaviour; after it returns no further callback is made for that registration) |

</details>

| `D3D12XboxCreateDevice` | implemented | [crates/compat/d3d12_x/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/d3d12_x/src/lib.rs) (console 0x14b0: NULL parameters -> E_INVALIDARG, *ppDevice cleared, ProcessDebugFlags (+4) stored as the process flags; a NULL ppDevice still creates through a local out and returns the creation HRESULT (no S_FALSE); the 0x28-byte Xbox One parameters are copied into a zeroed 0x48-byte Scarlett block with version 0x20004 and handed to the emulator d3d12_xs.dll; host D3D12CreateDevice if d3d12_xs is absent; tests::create_device_null_out_returns_the_creation_result_like_console) |
| `D3D12SerializeRootSignature` | implemented | [crates/compat/d3d12_x/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/d3d12_x/src/lib.rs) (host d3d12.dll after clearing the Xbox-only root flags (0x01000000, 0x02000000, 0x80000000) from a copy of the description; the bits are put back into the returned RTS0 blob, as d3d12_xs does; rs_strip_tests) |

</details>

## Trackmania imports (1.6.2.0)

Nadeo GBX engine (ManiaPlanet lineage), native C++ GameCore **Xbox One** build (`TargetDeviceFamily="XboxOne"`, PDB folder `Release_Gaming.Xbox.XboxOne.x64`, build id `2024-04-30_c8c94a9ed_..._XB1`), GDK edition string `October 2022 GRDK` in the EXE, Ubisoft UbiServices SDK 2020.Release.62. Scanned: `Mount` (GbxGame.exe, GameChat2.dll 2210, XCurl.dll 2210, MSVC 14.29 CRT) and `Mount/aegir` (aegir.dll, 36 MB, a WebKit/JavaScriptCore UI engine: 12,887 `WebCore`/`JSC` exports). GameChat2.dll and aegir.dll are not in GbxGame.exe's import table; they are loaded at run time (the EXE carries `aegir.dll` loader strings). Imports `d3d12_x.dll` / `xg_x.dll` / `dxcompiler_x.dll` (Xbox One variants, not the Scarlett `_xs` ones); the emulator provides them as separate crates with the console's export ordinals, and the remaining gaps are stubs. XGameRuntime is loaded by name.

<details><summary><b>api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll</b>: 2 functions; implemented 2</summary>

| Function | Status | Implemented in / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `RegisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f80 in the Fortnite image (file version 10.0.19041.3948). XDefiant image (10.0.22000.4429): thunk 0x5ad0 to register core 0x56ac, spot-checked to the same shape (*Registration = 0, 0x48-byte entry, shared counter increment, *Registration = id, return 0) and the same four WNF state names; its unregister and dispatch cores were not re-walked, and it imports RtlWaitOnAddress/RtlWakeAddressAll/NtQueryWnfStateData where Fortnite imports WaitOnAddress/WakeByAddressAll/RtlQueryWnfStateData. Fortnite thunk 0x4f80 -> register core 0x4b80 with the constrained list (.data 0xa930). Signature ULONG (PAPPCONSTRAIN_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION* Registration). Core: *Registration = 0 first, then under the module critical section (0xa900) a first-use init that subscribes the four PLM WNF state names (0x4a1c: RtlQueryWnfStateData then RtlSubscribeWnfStateChangeNotification, error -> RtlNtStatusToDosError returned); allocates a 0x48-byte entry (refcount 1 at +0x38, routine +0x20, context +0x28, id +0x30) or returns 8 (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); the id is one process-wide counter (0xa8a0) shared by both registration kinds, starting at 1; entry appended to the list tail; *Registration = id; returns 0. Here: compat-common os_host::plm::register(Constrained) in the os_host owner module (all compat DLLs must be built from the same compat-common so the owner publishes the register entry) (cross-module through TOMBOX_OSHOST_PLM_REGISTER), the same id counter, list and refcount/active-thread scheme. The emulator PLM signals (window minimise/restore watcher, TOMBOX_PLM_DIR command file, plm::set_constrained) stand in for the WNF names 0x41c60d2aa3bc2875 (constrained TRUE) and 0x41c60d2aa3bc3075 (constrained FALSE). Known deviation: the console writes *Registration without a NULL check (a NULL pointer faults); here NULL returns 87 (ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) and registers nothing) |
| `UnregisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4fa0 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5af0) -> unregister core 0x4dd8 with the constrained list. VOID (PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION Registration) on the console; the exported function returns a ULONG here (0, or 0x490 ERROR_NOT_FOUND) which a VOID caller ignores. Core: if never initialised returns at once; else under the lock finds the entry whose id (+0x30) matches in this kind's list (an id of the other kind is not found: wil log, 0x490), unlinks it, leaves the lock, then WaitOnAddress until the entry's active-thread field (+0x3c) is 0 or the calling thread (so an in-flight callback finishes first, and unregistering from inside the callback does not deadlock), drops the reference, and when both lists are empty unsubscribes the WNF names (RtlUnsubscribeWnfNotificationWaitForCompletion) and clears the init flag. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(Constrained) in the owner, same find/unlink/wait-for-in-flight behaviour; after it returns no further callback is made for that registration) |

</details>

## Minecraft imports (1.18.3064.0 Preview, Scarlett)

Minecraft Preview (Bedrock) GameCore **Scarlett** build (`TargetDeviceFamily="Scarlett"` in MicrosoftGame.Config), title id 639AA8DD. Scanned: the package root (Minecraft.Windows.exe, XCurl.dll, fmod.dll, v8, cohtml / RenoirCore UI, nvngx_dlss, dxcompiler / dxil, MSVC 14.x CRT); `data/` holds no PE files. `Minecraft.Windows.exe` is a patched copy of `Minecraft.Windows.exe.original` with an identical import table. No EmbeddedXvd was extracted (only `gameos.xvd`), so the title has no console-OS export table of its own: console attribution uses the union of the other titles' EmbeddedXvd, and the OS build used for per-build tables (22000) comes from the `10.0.22000.3160` GRDK link string in the EXE (October 2021 / March 2022 GRDK), not from the console OS. XGameRuntime is loaded by name (`xgameruntime.dll` + `QueryApiImpl`, 124 call sites in the EXE and 16 in XCurl.dll by the analysis/gdk scanner).

Static analysis only: this title has not been run.

Listed: imports from Xbox console libraries only. Not listed: 2086 imports a PC System32 DLL or API set provides, 1363 imports from DLLs shipped with the title.

<details><summary><b>api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll</b>: 2 functions; implemented 2</summary>

| Function | Status | Implemented in / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `UnregisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4fa0 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5af0) -> unregister core 0x4dd8 with the constrained list. VOID (PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION Registration) on the console; the exported function returns a ULONG here (0, or 0x490 ERROR_NOT_FOUND) which a VOID caller ignores. Core: if never initialised returns at once; else under the lock finds the entry whose id (+0x30) matches in this kind's list (an id of the other kind is not found: wil log, 0x490), unlinks it, leaves the lock, then WaitOnAddress until the entry's active-thread field (+0x3c) is 0 or the calling thread (so an in-flight callback finishes first, and unregistering from inside the callback does not deadlock), drops the reference, and when both lists are empty unsubscribes the WNF names (RtlUnsubscribeWnfNotificationWaitForCompletion) and clears the init flag. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(Constrained) in the owner, same find/unlink/wait-for-in-flight behaviour; after it returns no further callback is made for that registration) |
| `RegisterAppConstrainedChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-1.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f80 in the Fortnite image (file version 10.0.19041.3948). XDefiant image (10.0.22000.4429): thunk 0x5ad0 to register core 0x56ac, spot-checked to the same shape (*Registration = 0, 0x48-byte entry, shared counter increment, *Registration = id, return 0) and the same four WNF state names; its unregister and dispatch cores were not re-walked, and it imports RtlWaitOnAddress/RtlWakeAddressAll/NtQueryWnfStateData where Fortnite imports WaitOnAddress/WakeByAddressAll/RtlQueryWnfStateData. Fortnite thunk 0x4f80 -> register core 0x4b80 with the constrained list (.data 0xa930). Signature ULONG (PAPPCONSTRAIN_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPCONSTRAIN_REGISTRATION* Registration). Core: *Registration = 0 first, then under the module critical section (0xa900) a first-use init that subscribes the four PLM WNF state names (0x4a1c: RtlQueryWnfStateData then RtlSubscribeWnfStateChangeNotification, error -> RtlNtStatusToDosError returned); allocates a 0x48-byte entry (refcount 1 at +0x38, routine +0x20, context +0x28, id +0x30) or returns 8 (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); the id is one process-wide counter (0xa8a0) shared by both registration kinds, starting at 1; entry appended to the list tail; *Registration = id; returns 0. Here: compat-common os_host::plm::register(Constrained) in the os_host owner module (all compat DLLs must be built from the same compat-common so the owner publishes the register entry) (cross-module through TOMBOX_OSHOST_PLM_REGISTER), the same id counter, list and refcount/active-thread scheme. The emulator PLM signals (window minimise/restore watcher, TOMBOX_PLM_DIR command file, plm::set_constrained) stand in for the WNF names 0x41c60d2aa3bc2875 (constrained TRUE) and 0x41c60d2aa3bc3075 (constrained FALSE). Known deviation: the console writes *Registration without a NULL check (a NULL pointer faults); here NULL returns 87 (ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER) and registers nothing) |

</details>

| `XApuDequeueResult` | implemented | [crates/compat/xapu/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/xapu/src/lib.rs) (results commit in submission order, result event signalled on commit) |
| `XApuDisconnect` | implemented | [crates/compat/xapu/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/xapu/src/lib.rs) (waits for in-flight host decodes, then frees) |

### Minecraft: XCurl.dll

<details><summary><b>api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll</b>: 2 functions; implemented 2</summary>

| Function | Status | Implemented in / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `UnregisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f60 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5ab0) -> the same unregister core 0x4dd8 with the app-state list; same behaviour and the same ULONG-for-VOID note as ordinal 3. Here: os_host::plm::unregister(AppState) in the owner) |
| `RegisterAppStateChangeNotification` | implemented | api-ms-win-core-psm-appnotify-l1-1-0.dll -> console host minappmodelx.dll; [crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs](https://github.com/drycheetah84/TomBox/blob/master/crates/compat/minappmodelx/src/lib.rs) (console thunk RVA 0x4f40 (Fortnite image; XDefiant thunk 0x5a90) -> the same register core 0x4b80 with the app-state list (.data 0xa988). Signature ULONG (PAPPSTATE_CHANGE_ROUTINE Routine, PVOID Context, PAPPSTATE_REGISTRATION* Registration); routine is VOID (BOOLEAN Quiesced, PVOID Context). Same core as ordinal 1: shared id counter, entry appended, *Registration = id, returns 0. Delivery (WNF callback 0x48f0 -> dispatch 0x4790): WNF 0x41c60d2aa3bc1875 -> Quiesced = TRUE (suspend), 0x41c60d2aa3bc2075 -> Quiesced = FALSE (resume); every registration of that kind is snapshotted under the lock, addref'd and marked with the delivering thread id (+0x3c); one registration is called directly on the notification thread (0x45cc), several are fanned out to threadpool work items in a cleanup group and waited for (0x4650), falling back to sequential calls if the threadpool fails. Here: os_host::plm::register(AppState) in the owner; plm::suspend/resume (and the suspend timeout watch in os_host::quiesce) deliver on a separate notification thread, one registration inline and several in parallel threads joined before the signal returns. Same NULL-Registration deviation as ordinal 1) |

</details>

Minecraft: no EmbeddedXvd extracted, so no console OS export table of its own; per-build emulator tables and API-set hosts use build 22000 (from 10.0.22000.3160, not a console OS version).

## Xbox console libraries (EmbeddedXvd, union of all analysed titles)

Console-related exports of the DLLs in each title's EmbeddedXvd `Windows\System32` and `System32\forwarders` (union, so the newest console OS defines the surface; see Scope: exports a same-named PC System32 DLL also has are not counted), plus the GDK wrappers the emulator redirects inside the Fortnite EXE (statically linked, not exports; build-specific). The emulator's `mfplat.dll` proxy shadows the PC one for the title, so console mfplat exports it does not forward are counted missing. xgameruntime.dll exports only its dispatcher entry points; the GDK services behind it (XGameSave, XGameUi, XNetworking, ...) are reached via `QueryApiImpl` and are not enumerated here; its exports all exist in the PC xgameruntime.dll, so it is not counted. Import attribution for API-set imports uses the console host from `console_apiset_hosts.txt` when that table lists the function for the title's OS build, otherwise one host DLL (KernelBase, ucrtbase, ntdll, combase, ... first). Export names found as GetProcAddress strings in a title binary that also names the DLL count as title use. Emulator tables with per-OS-build variants (kernel32legacy `exports_19041.txt`) are applied per title build; a union row takes the lowest status across the builds that have the export.

### Not targeted (no analysed title references them)

The console-library total above (2155 exports in 110 libraries) keeps every console-only export in the denominator, including devkit, tools and OS service DLLs that no analysed title imports. 108 libraries (2153 exports) are imported by at least one title, directly or through API-set host attribution, or are emulator-provided; the 2 below (2 exports) are not targeted: no title EXE or shipped DLL imports any of their exports and the emulator does not provide them. They stay `missing` in the headline instead of being dropped from it, so the percentage is not inflated; a library moves out of this list as soon as a title imports it or an emulator crate exports it. The earlier Xbox-only figure (2078 exports) counted only DLLs with no same-named PC DLL and is kept in `summary.json` under `all_including_pc`. Coverage inside the 108 targeted libraries alone: 2132/2153.

| Library | Folder | Same-named PC DLL | Console-only exports | Present in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ConIoSrv.dll | System32 | no | 1 | Fortnite, XDefiant, Terraria, Trackmania |
| EventAggregation.dll | System32 | yes | 1 | Fortnite, XDefiant, Terraria, Trackmania |

### Evidence files

`sync.py` copies these from TomBox `analysis/progress_evidence/` into `tools/progress/data/evidence/`; `gen.py` reads them, and a missing file changes nothing. Schema: `{"title": "Fortnite", "source": "<log or test>", "modules": {"<Module.dll>": {"loaded": true, "resolved": ["<export>", ...], "unresolved": []}}}`. A module counts as loading and resolving only when `loaded` is true and `unresolved` is an empty list; a system-functions import flips to done only when its name is in `resolved`. `oshost.json` is `{"file": "<crate path>", "hooks": {"<console or PC twin DLL>": ["<export>", ...]}}`.

| File | Used for | Present |
|---|---|---|
| `chatprobe.json` | Fortnite GameChat2.dll / CONCRT140.dll leave the unverified lists; GameChat2 delay import in system functions | yes |
| `media.json` | MFPlat.DLL x16 and MFReadWrite.dll x2 in system functions | yes |
| `gameinput_ext.json` | GameInput.dll imports outside the EXE (TerrariaNative.dll) | no |
| `oshost.json` | PC-twin rows become `hooked` instead of `pc` | yes |

### Exports not present in every title's console OS

Newer console OS builds add exports; each is its own item in the map and stays `missing` until implemented.

## Unverified

- Fortnite: GameChat2.dll loads and resolves (chatprobe evidence, `tools/progress/data/evidence/chatprobe.json`).
- Fortnite: CONCRT140.dll loads and resolves (chatprobe evidence).
- GameInput.dll ordinal imports: #1: console GameInput.dll ordinal 1 = GameInputCreate on 19041.3948 / 22000.4429 / 22621.1852 (pefile).
- XAudio2_9.dll ordinal imports: #1: XAudio2Create: console XAudio2_9.dll export ordinal 1 on 10.0.19041.3948 (Fortnite EmbeddedXvd, rva 0x3d50, shared with #8 XAudio2CreateV2_9) and 10.0.22000.4429 (XDefiant EmbeddedXvd, rva 0x3d80) (pefile); imported by ordinal by FortniteClient-XSX-Shipping.exe (IAT 0x146155a40), EOSSDK-XboxOneGDK-Shipping.dll (0x180c36880), GbxGame.exe (Trackmania, 0x141ad6780), Terraria.exe (0x141459458), bink2scarlett.dll (XDefiant, 0x18002b030); crates/compat/xaudio2_9 exports XAudio2Create at #1 (build.rs .def, checked by tests/oracle.rs and tests/cdylib.rs) with console flag/processor handling; #2: CreateAudioReverb: console ordinal 2 on 19041.3948 (rva 0x3efc0) and 22000.4429 (rva 0x414d0); imported by Fortnite (IAT 0x146155a28) and Trackmania GbxGame.exe (0x141ad6768); compat DLL #2 forwards to the host System32 CreateAudioReverb (same shared XAudio2 codebase, no Xbox-only arguments); #4: CreateFX: console ordinal 4 on 19041.3948 (rva 0x63b50) and 22000.4429 (rva 0x66220); imported by Fortnite only (IAT 0x146155a38); compat DLL #4 forwards to the host CreateFX and logs the console-only GCXAudioExt loudness-meter CLSID when the host refuses it; #5: X3DAudioCalculate: console ordinal 5 on 19041.3948 (rva 0x3e920) and 22000.4429 (rva 0x40e60); imported by Fortnite (IAT 0x146155a48) and Trackmania (0x141ad6778); compat DLL #5 forwards to host X3DAudioCalculate; tests/oracle.rs runs console and compat X3DAudio side by side on packed structs and compares outputs; #6: X3DAudioInitialize: console ordinal 6 on 19041.3948 (rva 0x3d310) and 22000.4429 (rva 0x3f880); imported by Fortnite (IAT 0x146155a30) and Trackmania (0x141ad6770); compat DLL #6 forwards to host X3DAudioInitialize (instance layout identical, verified in tests/oracle.rs); map: console = PC = compat: 1 XAudio2Create, 2 CreateAudioReverb, 3 CreateAudioVolumeMeter, 4 CreateFX, 5 X3DAudioCalculate, 6 X3DAudioInitialize, 7 CreateAudioReverbV2_8, 8 XAudio2CreateV2_9, 9 XAudio2CreateWithVersionInfo, 10 XAudio2CreateWithSharedContexts (host 10.0.26100.9818 has the same ordinals); no title imports #3 or #7-#10.
- Ordinal imports (XAudio2_9 #1/#2/#4/#5/#6, GameInput #1) are resolved to names with the console DLL of the importing title's own EmbeddedXvd, then classified like named imports: XAudio2_9 against crates/compat/xaudio2_9 (its table is checked against the built target/release/XAudio2_9.dll), GameInput against the PC DLL. A title without an EmbeddedXvd (Minecraft) keeps the `#N` form. XAudio2 starts streaming in the Fortnite log, but there is no audio.
- GetProcAddress-string usage: an export name counts as used by a title when a binary of that title holds it as an exact ASCII string (not preceded by a printable byte, NUL-terminated) and also names the DLL (ASCII or UTF-16). It marks the console row as imported (lighter colour, targeted), but it is not a load observed at run time and does not enter the per-title import tables.
- API-set imports whose console host (`crates/compat/compat-common/data/console_apiset_hosts.txt`, per OS build with the runtime's nearest-lower-build rule) is minuserx, advapi32legacy, kernel32legacy or minappmodelx are listed against that host; API-set functions the host table does not list keep the PC name-match treatment, and that table is partial.
- PC-twin rows (minuserx -> user32, advapi32legacy -> advapi32, minappmodelx -> twinapi.appcore / kernel.appcore, for whichever of them has no emulator crate) are export-name matches on the twin only; the console DLLs' own behaviour is not reproduced. kernel32legacy is emulated by crates/compat/kernel32legacy and has no PC-twin rows.
- PC-provided is an export-name match (ordinal match for ordinal-only exports) against this PC's System32; behaviour differences between console and PC system DLLs are not measured, and such exports are outside this map.
- Titles without run evidence are classified statically only.
- Milestones are counted live by gen.py (mapping milestones from this map's sections, run milestones from `progress.json`); see `docs/MILESTONES.md`.
